6"></path></svg></a></div></div></section><section id="how" class="rd-section"><div class="rd-wrap"><div class="rd-section-hdr"><span class="rd-eyebrow">How an edge looks</span><h2>Three steps. One honest number.</h2><p>The AI computes &quot;true&quot; odds from millions of historical matches. Then it compares those to every bookmaker&#x27;s current price. When a price is too generous, you have an edge.</p></div><div class="rd-how"><div class="rd-how-step"><h3>Model the match</h3><p>The AI ingests team form, lineups, injuries, rest, venue and historical head-to-head — then outputs a probability distribution across every outcome.</p></div><div class="rd-how-step"><h3>Scan 40+ bookmakers</h3><p>We pull live odds from every book we follow. Each price implies a different probability.</p></div><div class="rd-how-step"><h3>Surface the edge</h3><p>When the bookmaker&#x27;s implied probability is lower than ours, you&#x27;ve got positive expected value. Only those bets appear in the value-bets stream.</p><span class="rd-how-tag">Edge = <!-- -->+<!-- -->11.56<!-- -->%</span></div></div></div></section><section id="value-bets" class="rd-section"><div class="rd-wrap"><div class="rd-section-hdr"><span class="rd-eyebrow">What you get</span><h2>Built for bettors who care about EV.</h2><p>Not a tipster. Not a hype channel. A disciplined value-bet engine with the math exposed.</p></div><div class="rd-edges"><div class="rd-edge-card"><div class="icn"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 2v4"></path><path d="M12 18v4"></path><path d="m4.93 4.93 2.83 2.83"></path><path d="m16.24 16.24 2.83 2.83"></path><path d="M2 12h4"></path><path d="M18 12h4"></path><path d="m4.93 19.07 2.83-2.83"></path><path d="m16.24 7.76 2.83-2.83"></path></svg></div><h3>AI-powered true odds</h3><p>Trained on millions of matches across every major league. Retrained continuously.</p></div><div class="rd-edge-card"><div class="icn"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="m2 12 5.25 5 2.625-2.5"></path><path d="M16 12h6"></path><path d="M4 12h6"></path><path d="M22 12l-5.25 5-2.625-2.5"></path></svg></div><h3>Only positive-edge bets</h3><p>We only surface bets where the mathematical advantage is verified. No guessing.</p></div><div class="rd-edge-card"><div class="icn"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><polyline points="12 6 12 12 16 14"></polyline></svg></div><h3>Continuous refresh</h3><p>Value windows close fast. Bookmakers adjust their lines constantly.
